package restaurantrush.boundary;

import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.PrintStream;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Scanner;

import restaurantrush.entity.Customer;
import restaurantrush.entity.CustomerStatus;
import restaurantrush.entity.HappyHour;
import restaurantrush.entity.Money;
import restaurantrush.entity.Order;
import restaurantrush.entity.Restaurant;
import restaurantrush.entity.Table;

/**
 * The command-line boundary. It prints the game state and reads the manager's choices as text;
 * it contains no game rules (the staff and domain objects decide whether a choice is legal) and
 * does not know the controller: the controller calls it, and Main wires the two together.
 */
public class RestaurantCLI {
    private final Scanner input;
    private final PrintStream out;
    private final boolean echoInput;

    /** echoInput repeats each line read, so a run fed from a file (stdin) reads like a transcript. */
    public RestaurantCLI(InputStream in, PrintStream out, boolean echoInput) {
        this.input = new Scanner(in);
        this.out = out;
        this.echoInput = echoInput;
    }

    public void displayIntro(int maxTurns) {
        out.println("SC2002 Restaurant Rush - Stage 3");
        out.println("You manage a restaurant for " + maxTurns + " turns. Each turn the Waiter does one");
        out.println("task (SEAT, TAKE_ORDER or SERVE), the Host can also SEAT one queued customer, and the Chef");
        out.println("advances one order by one unit (COOK). Once per game you may switch Happy Hour on.");
        out.println("Press Enter to WAIT. Goal: at least " + Restaurant.REQUIRED_PAID_CUSTOMERS + " paid customers, revenue of at least "
                + Money.format(Restaurant.REQUIRED_REVENUE_CENTS) + ", average satisfaction of at least "
                + Restaurant.REQUIRED_AVERAGE_SATISFACTION + ".");
    }

    /** Shown when the input ends before the game does. */
    public void displayInputEnded() {
        out.println();
        out.println("The input ended before the game finished, so the run stops here.");
    }

    public String readHostChoice() {
        return readLine("Host    [SEAT <#> | WAIT]: ");
    }

    public String readWaiterChoice() {
        return readLine("Waiter  [SEAT <#> | TAKE_ORDER <#> | SERVE <#> | WAIT]: ");
    }

    public String readChefChoice() {
        return readLine("Chef    [COOK <#> | WAIT]: ");
    }

    /** Asks whether to switch Happy Hour on now. Blank means no. */
    public boolean readHappyHourChoice() {
        while (true) {
            String answer = readLine("Happy Hour [y = switch on for this turn and the next | n or Enter = not now]: ")
                    .trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
            if (answer.isEmpty() || answer.equals("n") || answer.equals("no")) {
                return false;
            }
            if (answer.equals("y") || answer.equals("yes")) {
                return true;
            }
            displayError("'" + answer + "' is not y or n");
        }
    }

    public void displayTurnStatus(Restaurant restaurant, int turn, int maxTurns) {
        out.println();
        out.println("=================== TURN " + turn + " of " + maxTurns + " ===================");
        out.println("Revenue: " + Money.format(restaurant.getRevenueCents())
                + " | Average satisfaction of paid customers: " + averageText(restaurant)
                + " | Served: " + restaurant.getServedCustomerCount()
                + " | Unhappy departures: " + restaurant.getUnhappyDepartureCount());
        out.println("Happy Hour: " + happyHourText(restaurant.getHappyHour()));
        out.println("Waiting queue: " + queueText(restaurant));
        out.println("Tables: " + tablesText(restaurant));
        out.println("Customers:");
        if (restaurant.getCustomers().isEmpty()) {
            out.println("  (nobody has arrived yet)");
        }
        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {
            out.println(String.format(Locale.ROOT, "  %-4s %-8s satisfaction %3d  %-12s  order: %s",
                    "#" + customer.getId(), customer.getTypeLabel(), customer.getSatisfaction(), customer.getStatus(),
                    orderText(customer)));
        }
        out.println("Host tasks:   " + hostTasksText(restaurant));
        out.println("Waiter tasks: " + waiterTasksText(restaurant));
        out.println("Chef tasks:   " + chefTasksText(restaurant));
    }

    public void displayMessage(String message) {
        out.println("  * " + message);
    }

    public void displayError(String message) {
        String reason = message.endsWith(".") ? message.substring(0, message.length() - 1) : message;
        out.println("  ! Not possible: " + reason + ". Choose again.");
    }

    public void displayResult(boolean won, Restaurant restaurant, int turnsUsed) {
        out.println();
        out.println("=================== GAME OVER ===================");
        out.println(won ? "VICTORY" : "DEFEAT");
        out.println("Paid customers: " + restaurant.getPaidCustomerCount() + " (need "
                + Restaurant.REQUIRED_PAID_CUSTOMERS + ")");
        out.println("Revenue: " + Money.format(restaurant.getRevenueCents()) + " (need "
                + Money.format(Restaurant.REQUIRED_REVENUE_CENTS) + ")");
        out.println("Average satisfaction of paid customers: " + averageText(restaurant) + " (need "
                + Restaurant.REQUIRED_AVERAGE_SATISFACTION + ")");
        out.println("Customers served: " + restaurant.getServedCustomerCount()
                + " | Unhappy departures: " + restaurant.getUnhappyDepartureCount()
                + " | Turns used: " + turnsUsed);
    }

    private String readLine(String prompt) {
        out.print(prompt);
        out.flush();
        String line = input.nextLine();
        if (echoInput) {
            out.println(line);
        }
        return line;
    }

    private static String averageText(Restaurant restaurant) {
        if (restaurant.getPaidCustomerCount() == 0) {
            return "N/A";
        }
        return String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.1f", restaurant.getAverageSatisfaction());
    }

    private static String happyHourText(HappyHour happyHour) {
        if (happyHour.isActive()) {
            return "ACTIVE, " + happyHour.getRemainingTurns() + " turn(s) left including this one";
        }
        return happyHour.isAvailable() ? "available (switch on once per game)" : "used";
    }

    private static String queueText(Restaurant restaurant) {
        if (restaurant.getWaitingQueue().isEmpty()) {
            return "empty";
        }
        List<String> entries = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getWaitingQueue()) {
            entries.add("#" + customer.getId() + " " + customer.getTypeLabel() + " (satisfaction " + customer.getSatisfaction() + ")");
        }
        return String.join(" -> ", entries);
    }

    private static String tablesText(Restaurant restaurant) {
        List<String> entries = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Table table : restaurant.getTables()) {
            Customer occupant = table.getOccupied();
            entries.add("Table " + table.getTableId() + ": "
                    + (occupant == null ? "free"
                            : "#" + occupant.getId() + " " + occupant.getTypeLabel() + " (" + occupant.getStatus() + ")"));
        }
        return String.join(" | ", entries);
    }

    private static String orderText(Customer customer) {
        Order order = customer.getOrder();
        if (order == null) {
            return "-";
        }
        return order.getItem().getName() + " " + order.getCompletedUnits() + "/"
                + order.getItem().getPreparationUnits() + " units, " + order.getStatus()
                + ", " + Money.format(order.getBillCents());
    }

    private static String hostTasksText(Restaurant restaurant) {
        List<Customer> seatable = restaurant.getSeatableCustomers();
        return seatable.isEmpty() ? "none available (WAIT)" : "SEAT " + ids(seatable);
    }

    private static String waiterTasksText(Restaurant restaurant) {
        List<String> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
        List<Customer> seatable = restaurant.getSeatableCustomers();
        if (!seatable.isEmpty()) {
            tasks.add("SEAT " + ids(seatable));
        }
        List<Customer> toOrder = restaurant.getCustomersWithStatus(CustomerStatus.SEATED);
        if (!toOrder.isEmpty()) {
            tasks.add("TAKE_ORDER " + ids(toOrder));
        }
        List<Customer> toServe = restaurant.getCustomersToServe();
        if (!toServe.isEmpty()) {
            tasks.add("SERVE " + ids(toServe));
        }
        return tasks.isEmpty() ? "none available (WAIT)" : String.join("; ", tasks);
    }

    private static String chefTasksText(Restaurant restaurant) {
        List<Customer> toCook = restaurant.getCustomersToCook();
        return toCook.isEmpty() ? "none available (WAIT)" : "COOK " + ids(toCook);
    }

    private static String ids(List<Customer> customers) {
        List<String> numbers = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Customer customer : customers) {
            numbers.add("#" + customer.getId());
        }
        return String.join(" ", numbers);
    }
}

296	
297	' ---------- layout skeleton (hidden links keep the rows tidy) ----------
298	' Smetana cannot route labelled same-rank links, so sideways links carry no mid-link label.
299	Restaurant -[hidden]right- GameController
300	GameController -[hidden]right- StaffActions
301	StaffActions -[hidden]right- ManagerCommand
302	RestaurantCLI -[hidden]right- ArrivalSchedule
303	Main -[hidden]right- RestaurantCLI
304	Host -[hidden]right- Table
305	Table -[hidden]right- Waiter
306	Waiter -[hidden]right- Chef
307	Chef -[hidden]right- Cashier
308	Cashier -[hidden]right- Menu
309	Customer -[hidden]right- Order
310	Order -[hidden]right- MenuItem
311	RegularCustomer -[hidden]right- VIPCustomer
312	VIPCustomer -[hidden]right- CriticCustomer
313	CriticCustomer -[hidden]right- CustomerStatus
314	
315	' ---------- control and boundary ----------
316	Main .down.> RestaurantCLI : <<create>>
317	Main .right.> GameController : <<create>>
318	GameController "1" -up-> "cli 1" RestaurantCLI
319	GameController "1" -up-> "schedule 1" ArrivalSchedule
320	GameController "1" -right-> "staff 1" StaffActions
321	GameController "1" -left-> "restaurant 1" Restaurant
322	StaffActions .right.> ManagerCommand
323	RestaurantCLI .down.> Restaurant : displays
324	ArrivalSchedule .down.> Customer : <<create>>\nRegular, VIP, Critic
325	
326	' ---------- composition (Restaurant owns these) ----------
327	Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" HappyHour : happyHour
328	Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Host : host
329	Restaurant "1" *-down- "2" Table : tables
330	Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Waiter : waiter
331	Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Chef : chef
332	Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Cashier : cashier
333	Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Menu : menu
334	
335	' ---------- aggregation (customers arrive and leave) ----------
336	' Multiplicities at the Customer end sit in the labels: three links meet at Customer's corner and end labels would overlap there.
337	Restaurant "1" o-down- Customer : customers [0..*]
338	Restaurant "1" o-down- Customer : waitingQueue [0..*]\n{ordered, FIFO}
339	
340	' ---------- menu ----------
341	Menu "1" o-down- "3..*" MenuItem : items
342	MenuItem <|-down- ComboMeal
343	' Inheritance and aggregation join the same two boxes, so the aggregation is coloured and its multiplicity sits in the label
344	ComboMeal o-[#B5541C,bold]up- MenuItem : components [2]
345	
346	' ---------- domain associations ----------
347	Table "1" -down-> Customer : occupant [0..1]
348	Customer "1" -right-> Order : order [0..1]
349	Order "0..*" -right-> "1" MenuItem
350	Customer -down-> CustomerStatus : status
351	Order -down-> OrderStatus : status
352	Customer <|-down- RegularCustomer
353	Customer <|-down- VIPCustomer
354	Customer <|-down- CriticCustomer
355	
356	' ---------- dependencies ----------
357	GameController .[#777777].> Cashier
358	GameController .[#777777].> HappyHour
359	GameController .[#777777].> Customer
360	StaffActions .[#777777].> Restaurant
361	StaffActions .[#777777].> Waiter
362	StaffActions .[#777777].> Host
363	StaffActions .[#777777].> Chef
364	StaffActions .[#777777].> Customer
365	ManagerCommand .[#777777].> Customer
366	Host .right.> Table
367	Waiter .left.> Table
368	Host .down.> Customer : seats
369	Waiter .down.> Customer : seats / serves
370	Waiter .down.> Order : <<create>>
371	Waiter .down.> Menu : reads
372	Waiter .up.> HappyHour : applyDiscount
373	Chef .down.> Order : advances
374	Cashier .down.> Customer : charges
375	Cashier .down.> Order : reads bill
376	Cashier .up.> Restaurant : recordPayment,\nreleaseTableOf
377	Customer .up.> Menu : chooses from
378	
379	' ---------- annotations ----------
380	note top of Restaurant
381	  <b>COMPOSITION</b>
382	  Restaurant creates and owns its Tables, Menu,
383	  HappyHour and staff; they exist only as part of
384	  one Restaurant. Customers are aggregated:
385	  they arrive and leave. The waitingQueue and
386	  customers links both point at Customer.
387	end note
388	
389	note right of StaffActions
390	  <b>RESPONSIBILITY SPLIT</b>
391	  GameController only sequences the six steps.
392	  ManagerCommand reads one typed choice.
393	  StaffActions carries it out with the Host, Waiter
394	  or Chef and returns the sentence to show; the
395	  staff and domain objects reject what is illegal.
396	  Restaurant answers what each staff member can do
397	  now (getSeatableCustomers(), getCustomersToServe(),
398	  getCustomersToCook()), so RestaurantCLI only prints.
399	end note
400	
401	note bottom of Order
402	  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
403	  status is private and changes only through
404	  advancePreparation(), markServed(), markPaid(),
405	  cancel(); illegal transitions are rejected.
406	  Legal: PLACED → PREPARING → READY → SERVED → PAID
407	  (PLACED → READY directly for a 1-unit dish);
408	  PLACED / PREPARING / READY → CANCELLED if the
409	  customer leaves.
410	  billCents is set once in the constructor with no
411	  setter: the price is locked when ordered.
412	end note
413	
414	note bottom of CustomerStatus
415	  <b>CUSTOMER LIFECYCLE</b>
416	  WAITING → SEATED → ORDERED → SERVED →
417	  READY_TO_PAY → PAID; WAITING / SEATED / ORDERED
418	  → ABANDONED when satisfaction reaches 0.
419	  Served, eating and READY_TO_PAY customers
420	  lose no satisfaction.
421	end note
422	
423	note left of Customer
424	  <b>POLYMORPHISM</b>
425	  getSatisfactionLoss(), calculateBillCents(),
426	  chooseMenuItem() and onServed() are overridden.
427	  GameController and Waiter call them on Customer
428	  and never check the type.
429	  Loss per waiting turn: Regular 8, VIP 5, Critic 12.
430	  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
431	  satisfaction is private; subclasses change it only
432	  through adjustSatisfaction() (clamped 0..100).
433	  <b>TURN GUARDS</b>
434	  canFinishEating(turn): only after the serving turn.
435	  canPay(turn): only after the READY_TO_PAY turn.
436	end note
437	
438	note right of Waiter
439	  <b>BILLING</b>
440	  takeOrder(): bill = happyHour.applyDiscount(
441	    customer.calculateBillCents(price))
442	  VIP discount first, then Happy Hour; locked in
443	  the new Order. VIP Burger: 1500, 1350, 1080 cents.
444	end note
445	
446	note bottom of Restaurant
447	  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
448	  getWaitingQueue() is read-only; a customer leaves the
449	  queue only through seatFromQueue(), after the Waiter
450	  or Host has accepted the seating.
451	end note
452	
453	note right of HappyHour
454	  <b>HAPPY HOUR</b>
455	  Once per game, at turn start; active for that turn
456	  and the next. 20% off orders taken; +15 satisfaction
457	  (max 100) for customers waiting for a table or food.
458	end note
459	
460	note right of CriticCustomer
461	  <b>CRITIC RULE</b>
462	  Loses 12 per waiting turn. Always orders the most
463	  expensive menu item (Salad + Pasta, $24.30, 3 units).
464	  Served more than 2 turns after ordering: loses an
465	  extra 10 (onServed). A 3-unit combo cannot be served
466	  that fast, so this Critic always loses the extra 10.
467	end note
468	
469	note bottom of ComboMeal
470	  <b>COMBO</b>
471	  Hollow-triangle line: a ComboMeal is a MenuItem. Orange
472	  diamond line: it holds 2 MenuItem components (a MenuItem
473	  can be a component of 0..* combos).
474	  Stage 3 menu =Salad, Burger, Pasta + 2 ComboMeals
475	  (5 entries), registered once at game setup.
476	  Customer.chooseMenuItem(menu) picks from it.
477	  price = (a.price + b.price) x 90%, rounded:
478	  (sumCents x 90 + 50) / 100; units = a.units + b.units.
479	  Salad + Burger $21.60, 2 units; Salad + Pasta $24.30,
480	  3 units. Choice rule: customerId % menu.size(), so
481	  indexes 3 and 4 are the combos.
482	end note
483	
484	legend left
485	  <b>KEY</b>
486	  <back:#CCEBD2>  green  </back> new in Stage 3
487	  <back:#FAE8A6>  yellow  </back> changed since Stage 2
488	  <back:#DCEBF7>  blue  </back> unchanged
489	  <b>Changed since Stage 2</b>
490	  Customer: + recoverSatisfaction(), getDisplayName();
491	    isAwaitingService() is now public
492	  Waiter: takeOrder(...) also takes HappyHour
493	  MenuItem: no longer final (ComboMeal extends it)
494	  Restaurant: + host, happyHour, two ComboMeals on the
495	    menu; read-only waitingQueue with seatFromQueue(),
496	    staff task queries, hasMetObjective()
497	  Order: orderId and its static counter removed
498	  GameController: Host and Happy Hour steps; command
499	    parsing and task execution moved to ManagerCommand
500	    and StaffActions
501	  RestaurantCLI: Host and Happy Hour prompts; no longer
502	    references GameController (Main wires them)
503	end legend
504	@enduml
505	======
506	@startuml
507	' ======================================================================
508	' SC2002 Restaurant Rush - Stage 3 sequence diagram (source of truth)
509	' Scenario: a VIP orders a Burger while the manager has Happy Hour on.
510	' Turns 1-5 are unrolled so that "later turn" rules are visible:
511	'   T1 arrive/seat -> T2 order + Chef READY -> T3 serve -> T4 READY_TO_PAY -> T5 pay
512	' Turn 4 also has the base-setting arrival of customer 2, seated by the Host.
513	' Render: see docs/diagrams/src/README.md
514	' ======================================================================
515	title Restaurant Rush: Stage 3 sequence diagram (page 1 of 3)
516	mainframe **sd** VipOrdersBurgerDuringHappyHour
517	
518	skinparam style strictuml
519	skinparam defaultFontName SansSerif
520	skinparam defaultFontSize 12
521	skinparam shadowing false
522	skinparam ParticipantPadding 6
523	skinparam BoxPadding 4
524	skinparam sequenceMessageAlign center
525	skinparam responseMessageBelowArrow true
526	skinparam sequenceGroupHeaderFontStyle bold
527	skinparam sequenceGroupBackgroundColor #EEF3FA
528	skinparam sequenceGroupBodyBackgroundColor transparent
529	skinparam sequenceDividerBackgroundColor #E3EAF4
530	skinparam sequenceDividerBorderColor #7C93B8
531	skinparam sequenceDividerFontStyle bold
532	skinparam noteBackgroundColor #FFF4CF
533	skinparam noteBorderColor #B08A2E
534	skinparam noteFontSize 11
535	skinparam sequenceLifeLineBorderColor #999999
536	skinparam ArrowColor #222222
537	
538	actor Manager as M order 10
539	participant "main : Main" as main order 15
540	boundary "cli : RestaurantCLI" as cli order 20
541	control "game : GameController" as game order 30
542	control "staff : StaffActions" as staff order 33
543	control "cmd : ManagerCommand" as cmd order 36
544	entity "schedule : ArrivalSchedule" as sched order 40
545	entity "restaurant : Restaurant" as rest order 50
546	entity "hh : HappyHour" as hh order 60
547	entity "t1 : Table" as t1 order 70
548	entity "host : Host" as host order 85
549	entity "waiter : Waiter" as waiter order 90
550	entity "chef : Chef" as chef order 110
551	entity "cashier : Cashier" as cashier order 120
552	' vip (order 80) and order (order 100) are declared where they are created
553	
554	note over M, rest
555	  <b>Stage 3 | base setting (18 turns, 2 tables)</b>
556	  <b>Start:</b> turn 1, restaurant empty, 2 free tables. A VIP arrives
557	  (satisfaction 100). Burger = $15.00, 1 preparation unit.
558	  <b>Manager choice:</b> turn on Happy Hour at turn 1 or 2 (or never).
559	  <b>Outcome:</b> the VIP is PAID on turn 5, bill locked at <b>$10.80</b>,
560	  satisfaction <b>95</b>. If never activated: bill $13.50, satisfaction 90.
561	  <b>Host:</b> left at WAIT in turns 1-3 (the Waiter seats the VIP); in turn 4
562	  it seats customer 2, who is not traced further.
563	end note
564	
565	M -> main : run the game
566	main -> cli : <<create>> RestaurantCLI(in, out, echoInput)
567	main -> game : <<create>> GameController(cli)
568	note right of game : The constructor also creates the Restaurant, the ArrivalSchedule and StaffActions.\nStaffActions takes the HappyHour, the Menu or a staff member from the Restaurant\ngetters (getHappyHour(), getMenu(), getWaiter() ...); those calls are not drawn.\nEvery choice is read with ManagerCommand.parse(choice) (drawn where a task is done)
569	main -> game : startGame()
570	game -> cli : displayIntro(18)
571	
572	loop for each turn 1..18 (turns 1-5 shown)
573	
574	  == Turn 1: VIP arrives, manager turns Happy Hour on, Waiter seats the VIP ==
575	
576	  game -> game : **1 Arrivals** processScheduledArrival()
577	  game -> sched : arrival = isArrivalTurn(1)
578	  opt arrival
579	    game -> sched : vip = createArrival(1)
580	    create entity "vip : VIPCustomer" as vip order 80
581	    sched --> vip : <<create>>
582	    note right : status WAITING,\nsatisfaction 100
583	    game -> rest : registerArrival(vip)
584	    note right : vip joins\nwaitingQueue (FIFO)
585	  end
586	  game -> cli : displayTurnStatus(restaurant, 1, 18)
587	
588	  game -> game : **Start of turn** processHappyHourActivation()
589	  game -> hh : available = isAvailable()
590	  opt available (once per game)
591	    game -> cli : choice = readHappyHourChoice()
592	    cli -> M : Activate Happy Hour? (y/n)
593	    M --> cli : y
594	    alt choice == y
595	      game -> hh : activate()
596	      note right : used = true, remainingTurns = 2\n(this turn and the next)
597	    else choice == n
598	      note over game, hh : Declined now: Happy Hour stays available, so it can\nstill be switched on in turn 2 (same outcome here).\nNever activated: bill $13.50, satisfaction 90
599	    end
600	  end
601	
602	  game -> game : applyHappyHourRecovery()
603	  game -> hh : active = isActive()
604	  opt active
605	    game -> hh : amount = getRecoveryAmount()
606	    loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
607	      game -> vip : recoverSatisfaction(amount)
608	      note right : 100 stays 100 (capped)
609	    end
610	  end
611	
612	  game -> game : **2 Manager choices (Host)** coordinateHostAction()
613	  game -> cli : hostChoice = readHostChoice()
614	  cli -> M : Host: SEAT or WAIT?
615	  M --> cli : WAIT
616	  note right of game : The Host seats one queued customer on top of the Waiter's\ntask. WAIT here: the Waiter seats the VIP instead.
617	
618	  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
619	  note over game, cli : Steps 3-4 have nothing to do until a dish is READY\nor a customer is served (drawn from turn 3)
620	  note over game, cli : With several candidates the manager also picks which customer\nor order goes first; here there is only one candidate
621	  game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
622	  cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
623	  M --> cli : SEAT
624	  game -> cmd : cmd = parse(choice)
625	  game -> staff : message = waiter(cmd, 1)
626	  opt cmd.getVerb() == SEAT
627	    staff -> rest : queue = getWaitingQueue()
628	    staff -> cmd : vip = select(queue, customers, ...)
629	    note right : no number typed and one candidate:\nthe VIP is chosen
630	    staff -> rest : t1 = findAvailableTable()
631	    staff -> rest : seatFromQueue(vip, t1, waiter::seatCustomer)
632	    rest -> waiter : seatCustomer(vip, t1)
633	    waiter -> t1 : seat(vip)
634	    waiter -> vip : markSeated()
635	    note right : WAITING to SEATED
636	    note over rest : only after the Waiter accepted: vip leaves the waitingQueue
637	  end
638	  staff --> game : "Waiter seats Customer #1 (VIP) at Table 1."
639	  game -> cli : displayMessage(message)
640	  note over game, cli : Chef: manager picks WAIT (no order exists yet)
641	
642	  game -> game : **5 Waiting and abandonment** processEndOfTurnUpdates()
643	  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()
644	  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
645	    game -> vip : reduceSatisfaction()
646	    vip -> vip : adjustSatisfaction(-getSatisfactionLoss())
647	    note right : polymorphism: loss per waiting turn\nRegular 8, VIP 5, Critic 12\n100 to 95
648	    game -> vip : leave = shouldAbandon()
649	    opt leave (satisfaction == 0)
650	      game -> rest : handleAbandonment(vip)
651	      note right : cancel order, free table, ABANDONED
652	    end
653	  end
654	  game -> hh : endTurn()
655	  note right : remainingTurns 2 to 1
656	
657	  newpage Restaurant Rush: Stage 3 sequence diagram (page 2 of 3)
658	  == Turn 2: Happy Hour recovery, Waiter takes the order (price locked), Chef finishes it ==
659	
660	  game -> game : applyHappyHourRecovery()
661	  game -> hh : active = isActive()
662	  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
663	    game -> vip : recoverSatisfaction(15)
664	    note right : 95 to 100 (capped at 100)
665	  end
666	
667	  note over game, cli : Host: manager picks WAIT (no queued customer)
668	  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
669	  game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
670	  cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
671	  M --> cli : TAKE_ORDER
672	  game -> cmd : cmd = parse(choice)
673	  game -> staff : message = waiter(cmd, 2)
674	  opt cmd.getVerb() == TAKE_ORDER
675	    staff -> rest : seated = getCustomersWithStatus(SEATED)
676	    staff -> cmd : vip = select(seated, customers, ...)
677	    staff -> waiter : order = takeOrder(vip, menu, hh, 2)
678	    waiter -> vip : item = chooseMenuItem(menu)
679	    note right : documented rule: Burger\n(1500 cents, 1 unit)
680	    waiter -> vip : bill = calculateBillCents(1500)
681	    note right : polymorphism: VIPCustomer\noverrides it, 1500 x 0.9 = 1350
682	    waiter -> hh : bill = applyDiscount(1350)
683	    note right : Happy Hour active: 1350 x 0.8 = 1080\n(declined: stays 1350)
684	    create entity "order : Order" as order order 100
685	    waiter --> order : <<create>> Order(item, 1080, 2)
686	    note right : billCents set once in the\nconstructor, no setter:\nprice locked at 1080
687	    waiter -> vip : assignOrder(order)
688	    note right : SEATED to ORDERED
689	  end
690	  staff --> game : "Waiter takes the order ... Bill locked at $10.80."
691	  game -> cli : displayMessage(message)
692	  game -> cli : chefChoice = readChefChoice()
693	  cli -> M : Chef: choose an order or WAIT?
694	  M --> cli : COOK 1 (the VIP's order)
695	  game -> cmd : cmd = parse(chefChoice)
696	  game -> staff : message = chef(cmd)
697	  opt cmd.getVerb() == COOK
698	    staff -> rest : ordered = getCustomersWithStatus(ORDERED)
699	    staff -> cmd : vip = select(ordered, customers, ...)
700	    staff -> vip : order = getOrder()
701	    staff -> chef : prepare(order)
702	    chef -> order : advancePreparation()
703	    note right : Burger 1/1 unit: PLACED to READY.\nWaiter already acted this turn:\nserved on turn 3 at the earliest
704	  end
705	  staff --> game : "Chef works on the Burger ... order is READY."
706	  game -> cli : displayMessage(message)
707	
708	  game -> game : **5 Waiting and abandonment** processEndOfTurnUpdates()
709	  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
710	    game -> vip : reduceSatisfaction()
711	    note right : ORDERED, not yet served: 100 to 95\n(abandonment check as in turn 1)
712	  end
713	  game -> hh : endTurn()
714	  note right : remainingTurns 1 to 0\nHappy Hour is over
715	
716	  == Turn 3: Waiter serves the dish that became READY on an earlier turn ==
717	
718	  note over game, cli : Host: manager picks WAIT (no queued customer)
719	  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
720	  loop until the Waiter choice is accepted
721	    game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
722	    cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
723	    M --> cli : SERVE
724	    game -> cmd : cmd = parse(choice)
725	    game -> staff : message = waiter(cmd, 3)
726	    opt cmd.getVerb() == SERVE
727	      staff -> rest : ordered = getCustomersWithStatus(ORDERED)
728	      staff -> cmd : vip = select(ordered, customers, ...)
729	      staff -> waiter : serveOrder(vip, 3)
730	      waiter -> order : ready = isReady()
731	      alt ready (finished on turn 2)
732	        waiter -> order : markServed()
733	        note right : READY to SERVED
734	        waiter -> vip : markServed(3)
735	        note right : ORDERED to SERVED, servedTurn = 3
736	        waiter -> order : turnTaken = getTurnTaken()
737	        waiter -> vip : onServed(3 - turnTaken)
738	        note right : polymorphic hook: Critic loses 10 if served\nmore than 2 turns after ordering;\nVIP does nothing
739	        staff --> game : "Waiter serves the Burger to Customer #1 (VIP)."
740	        game -> cli : displayMessage(message)
741	      else not READY (for example, SERVE tried on turn 2)
742	        waiter --> staff : throws "order is not READY yet"
743	        staff --> game : the exception passes through
744	        game -> cli : displayError(reason)
745	        note right : staff task is not used up,\nthe manager chooses again
746	      end
747	    end
748	  end
749	  note over game, cli : Chef: manager picks WAIT
750	  note over game : **5** served customers lose no satisfaction,\nso the waiting loop has nothing to do
751	
752	  newpage Restaurant Rush: Stage 3 sequence diagram (page 3 of 3)
753	  == Turn 4: customer 2 arrives and the Host seats them; the VIP becomes READY_TO_PAY ==
754	
755	  game -> game : **1 Arrivals** processScheduledArrival()
756	  game -> sched : arrival = isArrivalTurn(4)
757	  opt arrival
758	    game -> sched : c2 = createArrival(4)
759	    game -> rest : registerArrival(c2)
760	    note right : customer 2 joins the waitingQueue\n(created as in turn 1; type set by the schedule)
761	  end
762	  game -> cli : displayTurnStatus(restaurant, 4, 18)
763	
764	  game -> game : **2 Manager choices (Host)** coordinateHostAction()
765	  game -> cli : hostChoice = readHostChoice()
766	  cli -> M : Host: SEAT or WAIT?
767	  M --> cli : SEAT
768	  game -> cmd : cmd = parse(hostChoice)
769	  game -> staff : message = host(cmd)
770	  opt cmd.getVerb() == SEAT
771	    staff -> rest : c2 = the only queued customer (select), t2 = findAvailableTable()
772	    staff -> rest : seatFromQueue(c2, t2, host::seatCustomer)
773	    rest -> host : seatCustomer(c2, t2)
774	    note right : Host.seatCustomer(c2, t2): markSeated() and Table.seat(c2), then Restaurant\nremoves c2 from the waitingQueue. The Waiter's task is not used
775	  end
776	  staff --> game : "Host seats Customer #2 (Regular) at Table 2."
777	  game -> cli : displayMessage(message)
778	  note over game, cli : Waiter and Chef: manager picks WAIT
779	
780	  game -> game : **4 Eating and payment** coordinatePaymentProcessing()
781	  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()
782	  loop for each customer (the vip is shown)
783	    game -> vip : eaten = canFinishEating(4)
784	    opt eaten (SERVED and 4 > servedTurn 3)
785	      game -> vip : markReadyToPay(4)
786	      note right : SERVED to READY_TO_PAY, readyTurn = 4
787	    end
788	  end
789	  game -> rest : payer = findOldestEligiblePayer(4)
790	  rest -> vip : canPay(4)
791	  note over game, cashier : false (4 > readyTurn 4 does not hold), so payer is null\nand the Cashier does not charge yet
792	  note over game, rest : **5** customer 2 is seated and unserved, so it loses its per-turn loss;\nthe VIP (READY_TO_PAY) loses nothing
793	
794	  == Turn 5: Cashier collects the locked bill ==
795	
796	  note over game, cli : Host: WAIT. Waiter takes customer 2's order and the Chef starts it\n(not traced further: this trace follows the VIP)
797	  game -> game : **4 Eating and payment** coordinatePaymentProcessing()
798	  note over game, rest : nobody is SERVED, so nobody new finishes eating
799	  game -> rest : payer = findOldestEligiblePayer(5)
800	  rest -> vip : canPay(5)
801	  note over game, cashier : true (5 > readyTurn 4): payer is the vip
802	  opt payer is not null
803	    game -> cashier : processPayment(vip, restaurant)
804	    cashier -> vip : order = getOrder()
805	    cashier -> order : bill = getBillCents()
806	    note right : 1080: the locked price,\nnot recalculated
807	    cashier -> rest : recordPayment(bill)
808	    note right : revenue += $10.80\nvip counts as paid with satisfaction 95
809	    cashier -> vip : markPaid()
810	    note right : READY_TO_PAY to PAID
811	    cashier -> order : markPaid()
812	    note right : SERVED to PAID
813	    cashier -> rest : releaseTableOf(vip)
814	    rest -> t1 : release()
815	  end
816	
817	  ... turns 6 to 18 run the same six steps for the other customers ...
818	end
819	
820	game -> rest : **6 Result** won = hasMetObjective()
821	note right : paid customers >= 4 and revenue >= 4500\nand average satisfaction >= 60, from its own books\n(the game never ends early)
822	game -> cli : displayResult(won, restaurant, 18)
823	alt won
824	  cli -> M : Victory
825	else not won
826	  cli -> M : Defeat
827	end

## 0061 User — 2026-11-03T09:20:03.991Z

Source record: 197; UUID: 1db6fc29-4c62-44ea-a3fc-4ce0026a1904.

### Tool result: toolu_01NKPQNtuXk8ZNogdyepc6cg

=========== Ryan/reflection/Individual Contribution Report.pdf
What did I learn?

I gained a better understanding of how OOP principles can be applied to accommodate
real life changing software requirements. I learned inheritance, polymorphism and
abstraction support the Open/Closed Principle by allowing new functionality to be
introduced without extensively modifying existing classes. I also improved my ability to
construct UML class diagrams and represent relationships between classes including
inheritance and associations. The project helped me see how they contribute to designing
a real system outside of only theory in lectures.

How did I apply it?

My main contribution was designing parts of Stage 2 and 3 UML class diagrams and
explaining how I implemented the SOLID principles.

For Stage 2, I focused on introducing VIPCustomer and CriticCustomer as subclasses of
the abstract Customer class. This allowed different customer types to have specialized
behaviors such as waiting satisfaction penalties, discounts and ordering preferences while
following the same general restaurant workflow.

For Stage 3, I focused on extending the menu system through ComboMeal which inherits
from MenuItem and contains two existing menu items. This design allows combo meals to
be treated as menu items withing the existing ordering structure.

These designs help me apple lecture content and understand the importance of designing
classes that can accommodate new requirements.

What challenged me?

One of the biggest challenges was determining how to incorporate the new requirements
into the existing class structure without creating unnecessary dependencies or modifying
too many classes. I initially found it difficult to decide whether new features should be
modelled as subclasses or handled by modifying existing classes.

Another challenge was constructing and presenting the UML diagrams. As more classes,
methods and relationships were introduced, the diagrams became increasingly
complicated and difficult to read. I also had limited experience using Google Colab and
Mermaid for UML diagrams, which made generating, formatting and exporting the
diagrams challenging.
How did I solve it?

To address the design challenges, I revisited the concepts of inheritance, polymorphism
and SOLID principles, particularly OCP and LSP. I examined how customer-specific
behaviors could be encapsulated within subclasses rather than implemented using
repeated conditional checks in the controller.

Similarly, for the Stage 3 menu extension, I used the existing MenuItem abstraction to
represent ComboMeal, allowing the design to reuse the ordering structure.

For the diagram-related difficulties, I experimented with different rendering and export
methods in Google Colab. After encountering issues with diagram scaling and image
generation, I exported the diagrams as PNG files instead of relying on screenshots. I also
organised the diagrams and design documentation for inclusion in the group's GitHub
repository and final report.

This experience taught me that designing extensible software requires more than applying
inheritance mechanically. It involves understanding each class's responsibility,
considering the impact of new requirements on existing components, and making design
decisions that maintain clarity and flexibility.
=========== Ryan/reflection/Stage 2 and 3 SOLID Principles.pdf
Design and SOLID Principles

Stage 2
S : Single Responsibility
Where it shows: every class owns one concern

    � ArrivalSchedule -> who arrives and when
    � HappyHour -> The Happy Hour rules and timer
    � Order -> One dish's preparation progress and its locked price
    � Host/Waiter/Chef/Cashier -> one staff role each
    � RestarauntCLI -> Input and output only
    � GameController -> Turn sequencing only

Without it, GameController would hold a happyHourTurnsLeft counter, a happyHourUsed
flag, the arrival table and the discount maths.

Happy Hour's rule live entirely in HappyHour. Changing the discount from 20% to 25%
touches one constant in one class.

O: Open/Closed (Open for extension and closed for modification)
Where it shows:
Stage 2 : adding VIP and Critics meant new subclasses. The waiting-loss loop, Cashier and
Chefs were not edited.

Stage 3: adding ComboMeal meant a new subclass of MenuItem. Chef, Order and Cashier
were not edited.
Without it, adding a fourth customer type would mean finding and editing every one of
these chains: satisfaction, billing and ordering.

I : Interface Segregation ( dont force a class to depend on methods it doesnt use)
Where it shows: you deliberately did not create one big Staff superclass. Each role exposes
only its own operations:
Host has seatCustomer only.
Chef has prepare only.

abstract class Staff {
  abstract void seatCustomer(...); abstract Order takeOrder(...);
  abstract void serveOrder(...); abstract void prepare(...);
  abstract void processPayment(...);

}
Then Host has to implement prepare() and processPayment() with empty bodies or
exceptions. Those are meaningless methods, and an LSP risk too.
.:

SC2002 Restaurant Rush - Stage 3
You manage a restaurant for 18 turns. Each turn the Waiter does one
task (SEAT, TAKE_ORDER or SERVE), the Host can also SEAT one queued customer, and the Chef
advances one order by one unit (COOK). Once per game you may switch Happy Hour on.
Press Enter to WAIT. Goal: at least 4 paid customers, revenue of at least $45.00, average satisfaction of at least 60.

=================== TURN 1 of 18 ===================
Happy Hour: available (switch on once per game)
Happy Hour [y = switch on for this turn and the next | n or Enter = not now]:   ! Not possible: 'maybe' is not y or n. Choose again.

Host    [SEAT <#> | WAIT]:   ! Not possible: Unknown Host command 'FLY'. Use SEAT or WAIT. Choose again.

Host    [SEAT <#> | WAIT]:   ! Not possible: There is no customer #9. Choose again.

Waiter  [SEAT <#> | TAKE_ORDER <#> | SERVE <#> | WAIT]:   * Waiter takes the order of Customer #1 (VIP). They choose Burger (list price $15.00, 1 preparation unit(s)). Bill locked at $13.50.
Chef    [COOK <#> | WAIT]:   ! Not possible: Unknown Chef command 'SEAT'. Use COOK or WAIT. Choose again.
Chef    [COOK <#> | WAIT]:   * Chef works on the Burger for Customer #1 (VIP): 1/1 units, order is READY.

=================== TURN 2 of 18 ===================

## 0076 Assistant — 2026-11-03T09:22:06.280Z

Source record: 250; UUID: 1f6229ea-36ef-4536-aee0-f85c254531a3.

**The project does not deserve full marks as submitted. I make it about 79/100 on what is in the repo, and nearly all of the missing marks are outside the code.**

I built each tag (`stage-1`, `stage-2`, `stage-3`) with `javac --release 17` and ran the tests and recorded runs at every tag. The tests pass at 55, 108 and 189 checks. The victory and defeat replays end as the README says. I probed bad input, which was rejected with a reason and did not use up the staff task. I also checked the README's changed-file lists against `git diff`, and they match exactly. I rendered all four diagram PDFs and looked at them.

## Mark sheet

| Criterion | Max | My mark | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| Class diagram | 25 | ~22 | Covers customer types, staff, queue, tables, orders, combo, Happy Hour and the CLI. Multiplicities are in link labels rather than at the line ends. Long dependency lines make it cluttered. A few details are missing from the diagram: `Money`, `Restaurant.getTables()`, `Order.getItem()`, `Order.getCompletedUnits()`. |
| Sequence diagram | 20 | ~19 | Full flow from arrival to payment and the result check, with the CLI, controller, staff and domain objects. It uses opt, alt and loop with guards. The only manager decision shown is switching Happy Hour on or off. |
| Design consideration | 15 | ~8 | The code is well split, and the diagram notes cover OO concepts. There is no SOLID discussion with code evidence, no trade-off section and no alternative design. The SOLID PDF in `docs/individual/Ryan/reflection/` has only S, O and I. It also lists Stage 3 classes (HappyHour, Host) under Stage 2. |
| Implementation | 20 | ~19–20 | Both endings reproduce. Customer behaviour is dispatched polymorphically with no `instanceof`. Combo and Happy Hour prices match the reference values. |
| Process | 20 | ~10 | Snapshots and history ~3/6. Change analysis ~5/5. Report and reflection ~2/4. Video 0/5. |

## What costs the marks

1. **No video.** The README timestamp table says "To be filled in" and has no link. That is 5 marks.
2. **No design discussion.** There is no PDF report in the repo, and the README has no section on SOLID or trade-offs. If the report exists in your zip and covers this, up to 7 marks come back.
3. **Reflections.** Only Ryan has one. The Li MU-EN, Peramudhan and Ye Fei reflection folders are empty. Their AI-history folders are empty too, except Li MU-EN's diagram sessions. There is no AI history for the session that wrote the Stage 1–3 code.
4. **History looks backfilled.** All stage commits were made between 02:57 and 03:14 on 3 Nov, and the six Stage 1 commits share one minute. The `stage-1` tag also contains the Stage 2 and 3 diagram files, because the diagrams were committed before the code. The rubric specifically asks for development history, not files added at the end.

## To reach full marks

- Record the 15-minute video and fill in the README timestamps. Use the seven segments from the assignment.
- Write the design section in the report.
  - Add a table of key abstractions and their responsibilities.
  - Cover S, O, L, I and D with code evidence. Fix the Stage 2 and 3 mix-up in Ryan's PDF.
  - Add one real alternative with its consequences, for example type checks in the controller versus customer hooks, or `Host` as a `Waiter` subclass versus a separate class.
- Have the other three members add reflections and chat histories.
- Don't change commit timestamps. Say in the README how the history was built.
- Optional polish, worth about a mark: have `StaffActions` use `getCustomersToServe()` and `getCustomersToCook()`, so `SERVE` or `COOK` with no number picks the right customer instead of asking for one. Add the missing items to the class diagram. Delete the 1 MB `stage-1-class.uml`. Remove the `puml` comment pointing to `docs/diagrams/src/README.md`, which is gitignored.

I could only mark what is in the repo. If a separate report PDF or video exists, the Design consideration, Report and reflection, and Recorded demonstration marks would change.

const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'rr-diagrams-'));
const sources = fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.puml'));

for (const file of sources) {
  const name = file.replace(/\.puml$/, '');
  execFileSync('java', ['-jar', jar, '-tsvg', '-charset', 'UTF-8', '-o', tmp, path.join(dir, file)], { stdio: 'inherit' });

  // PlantUML writes name.svg, then name_001.svg, name_002.svg, ... for each newpage.
  const pageFiles = fs
    .readdirSync(tmp)
    .filter((f) => new RegExp(`^${name}(_\\d{3})?\\.svg$`).test(f))
    .sort();
  const pages = pageFiles.map((f) => {
    const svg = fs.readFileSync(path.join(tmp, f), 'utf8');
    const [, , w, h] = svg.match(/viewBox="([\d.\s]+)"/)[1].trim().split(/\s+/).map(Number);
    return { svg: svg.replace(/^<\?xml[^>]*>/, ''), width: Math.ceil(w), height: Math.ceil(h) };
  });
  const width = Math.max(...pages.map((p) => p.width));

  // One named @page per diagram page, so each PDF page gets its own height.
  const html = path.join(tmp, `${name}.html`);
  fs.writeFileSync(
    html,
    `<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><style>` +
      pages.map((p, i) => `@page p${i}{size:${width}px ${p.height}px;margin:0}`).join('') +
      `html,body{margin:0;padding:0}svg{display:block}` +
      `.page{width:${width}px;overflow:hidden;break-after:page}.page:last-child{break-after:auto}</style>` +
      pages.map((p, i) => `<div class="page" style="page:p${i};height:${p.height}px">${p.svg}</div>`).join(''),
  );

  const common = ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--hide-scrollbars'];
  execFileSync(chrome, [...common, '--no-pdf-header-footer', `--print-to-pdf=${path.join(outDir, `${name}.pdf`)}`, pathToFileURL(html).href], { stdio: 'ignore' });
  console.log(`${name}: ${pages.length} page(s), ${width}px wide, heights ${pages.map((p) => p.height).join('/')}px -> ${name}.pdf`);
}
fs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });

## 0081 User — 2026-11-03T09:25:13.052Z

### Tool result: toolu_01KnA3ZZD9z89UxVitEdFyEY

1	# Diagrams
2	
3	The `.puml` files in this folder (`docs/diagrams/src/`) are the sources of truth. The `.pdf` files one level up (`docs/diagrams/`) are generated (vector); do not edit them by hand.
4	
5	| Diagram | Source | PDF | Notes |
6	|---|---|---|---|
7	| Stage 1 class | `stage-1-class.puml` | `stage-1-class.pdf` | Redrawn in PlantUML from the Visual Paradigm model `stage-1-class.uml` (kept as the archive). Every class is new. |
8	| Stage 2 class | `stage-2-class.puml` | `stage-2-class.pdf` | Colour key: green new in Stage 2, yellow changed since Stage 1, blue unchanged. |
9	| Stage 3 class | `stage-3-class.puml` | `stage-3-class.pdf` | Colour key: green new in Stage 3, yellow changed since Stage 2, blue unchanged. |
10	| Stage 3 sequence | `stage-3-sequence.puml` | `stage-3-sequence.pdf` | A VIP orders a Burger with Happy Hour on. Turns 1 to 5 are drawn one by one over three pages (setup and turn 1; turns 2 and 3; turns 4 and 5 and the result). The Host is left at WAIT in turns 1 to 3 and seats customer 2 (the base-setting turn-4 arrival) in turn 4; customer 2 is not traced further. |
11	
12	Plain accessors that no other diagram uses are left out of the class diagrams. Operations that the sequence diagram calls are always listed, and every message in the sequence diagram is an operation declared in the Stage 3 class diagram.
13	
14	## Drawing conventions
15	
16	- Three links meet at the top-left corner of `Customer` (`customers`, `waitingQueue` from `Restaurant`, and `occupant` from `Table`), and end labels there overlapped. Their multiplicities at the `Customer` end are written in the link label instead (`customers [0..*]`, `occupant [0..1]`). The same is done for `Customer` to `Order` (`order [0..1]`).
17	- `occupant` is directed from `Table`, which holds the reference. A table has at most one customer, and a customer has a table only once seated, hence `[0..1]`.
18	- `ComboMeal` is both a `MenuItem` (hollow-triangle line) and made of two `MenuItem` components (orange diamond line, `components [2]`). Both lines join the same two boxes, so the aggregation is coloured and its multiplicity is in the label.
19	
20	## Regenerating
21	
22	You need Java, a Chromium-based browser and a PlantUML jar (1.2025.4 was used):
23	
24	```
25	node docs/diagrams/src/render.mjs path/to/plantuml.jar
26	```
27	
28	This renders every `.puml` in this folder to a vector `.pdf` in `docs/diagrams/`. A diagram that PlantUML splits with `newpage` becomes a multi-page PDF; every page is as wide as the widest page and as tall as its own diagram. Set `CHROME` if the script cannot find your browser. If you need a PNG or SVG for a document, export it from the PDF or run PlantUML directly; those formats are not kept in the repo.
29	
30	## Using the diagrams in the report
31	
32	The PDFs are drawn large so that nothing overlaps. At 100% the text is 9 pt, and the page sizes are:
33	
34	| PDF | Page size (pt) |
35	|---|---|
36	| `stage-1-class.pdf` | 1207 x 1460 |
37	| `stage-2-class.pdf` | 1481 x 1503 |
38	| `stage-3-class.pdf` | 1562 x 1979 |
39	| `stage-3-sequence.pdf` | 1792 wide; pages 1704, 1436 and 1484 tall |
40	
41	Shrunk to A4 landscape width (842 pt) the text drops to about 4 pt, which is too small to read in print. Give each diagram its own full A3 landscape page (about 66%, text about 6 pt), or place it full-width on a rotated A4 page and keep the vector PDF in the repo for zooming. Import the three sequence pages as separate figures rather than as one scaled page.
42	
43	The class diagrams use PlantUML's built-in Smetana layout, so Graphviz is not needed. Smetana cannot route labelled same-row links, so sideways links carry no mid-link label.
44	
45	## Keeping diagrams in step with the code
46	
47	Each diagram must match the code of the stage it describes. The class diagrams were aligned with the Java code at tags `stage-1`, `stage-2` and `stage-3`, and every member listed in a class diagram (name and visibility) was checked against the compiled classes of that tag. Before changing a stage, check again and re-render.
48	
49	Deliberate differences between the diagrams and the code: plain accessors and private helpers are not drawn (for example `Restaurant.getTables()`, `Table.getTableId()`, `Order.getItem()`, the controller's private task methods), and the `Money` formatting helper is not drawn.
50

let d=0, b=\"\"; for (const c of s){ if(\"<([\".includes(c)) d++; if(\">)]\".includes(c)) d--; if(c===sep && d===0){out.push(b); b=\"\";} else b+=c;} if(b.trim()) out.push(b); return out.map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean); }\nfunction walk(d){return fs.readdirSync(d,{withFileTypes:true}).flatMap(e=>e.isDirectory()?walk(path.join(d,e.name)):[path.join(d,e.name)]);}\nconst MODS = new Set([\"public\",\"private\",\"protected\",\"static\",\"final\",\"abstract\",\"synchronized\"]);\nconst code = {};\nfor (const f of walk(path.join(root,\"src\")).filter(f=>f.endsWith(\".java\"))) {\n  let src = fs.readFileSync(f,\"utf8\").replace(/\\/\\*[\\s\\S]*?\\*\\//g,\"\").replace(/\\/\\/.*$/gm,\"\");\n  const m = src.match(/\\b((?:public|final|abstract)\\s+)*(class|enum|interface)\\s+(\\w+)(?:\\s+extends\\s+(\\w+))?/);\n  if(!m) continue;\n  const name = m[3]; const members = [];\n  const body = src.slice(src.indexOf(\"{\", m.index));\n  let depth=0, buf=\"\", skipSemi=false;\n  const handle = (text, hasBlock) => {\n    text = text.trim().replace(/\\s+/g,\" \");\n    if(!text) return;\n    text = text.replace(/^(@\\w+(\\([^)]*\\))?\\s*)+/,\"\");\n    if (/(^| )(class|enum|interface) \\w+/.test(text) && hasBlock) return;\n    const toks = text.split(\" \");\n    const mods = []; let i=0; while(i<toks.length && MODS.has(toks[i])) mods.push(toks[i++]);\n    const rest = text.split(\" \").slice(i).join(\" \");\n    const vis = mods.includes(\"public\")?\"+\":mods.includes(\"private\")?\"-\":mods.includes(\"protected\")?\"#\":\"~\";\n    const flags = {static:mods.includes(\"static\"), abstract:mods.includes(\"abstract\")};\n    const pm = rest.match(/^(?:(.+?)\\s+)?(\\w+)\\s*\\((.*)\\)\\s*(?:throws [\\w, ]+)?$/);\n    if (pm && !rest.includes(\"=\")) {\n      const [, ret, mname, params] = pm;\n      const types = splitTop(params).map(p => { const t=p.trim(); const ix=t.lastIndexOf(\" \"); return ix<0? t : t.slice(0,ix).replace(/^final /,\"\"); });\n      members.push({kind: ret? \"op\":\"ctor\", vis, ...flags, name:mname, types:types.map(norm), ret: ret? norm(ret):\"\"});\n      return;\n    }\n    const fm = rest.match(/^(.+?)\\s+(\\w+)\\s*(=.*)?$/);\n    if (fm) members.push({kind:\"field\", vis, ...flags, name:fm[2], types:[], ret:norm(fm[1])});\n  };\n  for (let i=0;i<body.length;i++){\n    const c = body[i];\n    if (c===\"{\"){\n      if (depth===1){\n        if (/=\\s*$/.test(buf)) { handle(buf.replace(/=\\s*$/,\"= x\"), false); skipSemi=true; buf=\"\"; }\n        else { handle(buf, true); buf=\"\"; }\n      }\n      depth++; continue;\n    }\n    if (c===\"}\"){ depth--; continue; }\n    if (depth===1){ if(c===\";\"){ if(skipSemi){skipSemi=false;} else handle(buf,false); buf=\"\"; } else buf+=c; }\n  }\n  code[name] = {members, isEnum:m[2]===\"enum\", ext:m[4]};\n}\n// ---- diagram\nconst lines = fs.readFileSync(puml,\"utf8\").split(/\\r?\\n/);\nconst dia = {}; let cur=null, pending=\"\";\nfor (const raw of lines){\n  const cm = raw.match(/^\\s*(?:(abstract)\\s+)?(class|enum)\\s+(\\w+)[^{]*\\{\\s*$/);\n  if (cm){ cur=cm[3]; dia[cur]={members:[], isEnum:cm[2]===\"enum\"}; continue; }\n  if (cur && /^\\s*\\}\\s*$/.test(raw)){ cur=null; continue; }\n  if (!cur || dia[cur].isEnum) continue;\n  let line = raw.trim(); if(!line) continue;\n  if (pending){ line = pending+\" \"+line; pending=\"\"; }\n  line = line.replace(/\\\\n/g,\" \");\n  let opens = (line.match(/\\(/g)||[]).length, closes=(line.match(/\\)/g)||[]).length;\n  if (opens>closes){ pending=line; continue; }\n  const stat = /\\{static\\}/.test(line), abs = /\\{abstract\\}/.test(line);\n  line = line.replace(/\\{(static|abstract)\\}\\s*/g,\"\").trim();\n  const vm = line.match(/^([+\\-#~])\\s*(.*)$/); if(!vm) continue;\n  const vis=vm[1]; const t=vm[2].trim();\n  const om = t.match(/^(\\w+)\\s*\\((.*)\\)\\s*(?::\\s*(.+))?$/);\n  if (om){\n    const types = splitTop(om[2]).map(p => { const ix=p.indexOf(\":\"); return norm(ix>=0? p.slice(ix+1): p); });\n    dia[cur].members.push({kind: om[3]? \"op\":\"ctor\", vis, static:stat, abstract:abs, name:om[1], types, ret: om[3]? norm(om[3]):\"\"});\n  } else {\n    const fm = t.match(/^(\\w+)\\s*:\\s*([^=]+?)\\s*(=.*)?$/);\n    if (fm) dia[cur].members.push({kind:\"field\", vis, static:stat, abstract:abs, name:fm[1], types:[], ret:norm(fm[2])});\n    else console.log(\"  (unparsed diagram line)\", cur, t);\n  }\n}\nconst sig = (m) => m.kind===\"field\" ? `${m.name}:${m.ret}` : `${m.name}(${m.types.join(\",\")})${m.ret?\":\"+m.ret:\"\"}`;\nlet problems = 0;\nconst names = new Set([...Object.keys(code), ...Object.keys(dia)]);\nfor (const n of [...names].sort()){\n  if(!code[n]){ console.log(`[diagram only] ${n}`); problems++; continue; }\n  if(!dia[n]){ console.log(`[class not in diagram] ${n}`); problems++; continue; }\n  if (code[n].isEnum) continue;\n  const c = code[n].members, d = dia[n].members; const msgs=[];\n  const dmap = new Map(d.map(m=>[sig(m), m]));\n  const cmap = new Map(c.map(m=>[sig(m), m]));\n  for (const [k,m] of cmap){ const x = dmap.get(k); if(!x) msgs.push(`  missing in diagram: ${m.vis}${m.static?\"{s}\":\"\"}${m.abstract?\"{a}\":\"\"} ${k}`); else { if (x.vis!==m.vis) msgs.push(`  visibility ${k}: code ${m.vis} diagram ${x.vis}`); if (!!x.static!==!!m.static) msgs.push(`  static flag ${k}: code ${m.static} diagram ${x.static}`); if (!!x.abstract!==!!m.abstract) msgs.push(`  abstract flag ${k}`);} }\n  for (const [k,m] of dmap){ if(!cmap.has(k)) msgs.push(`  not in code: ${m.vis} ${k}`); }\n  if (msgs.length){ console.log(n+\":\"); msgs.forEach(x=>console.log(x)); problems+=msgs.length; }\n}\nconsole.log(problems? `${problems} difference(s)`: \"diagram matches code\");\nEOF\nfor s in 3; do echo \"=== stage $s\"; node \"$SP/compare2.mjs\" \"$SP/wt/s$s\" \"D:/Java Projects/SC2002GroupProject/docs/diagrams/src/stage-$s-class.puml\"; done 2>&1 | head -150",

@startuml
' ======================================================================
' SC2002 Restaurant Rush - Stage 1 class diagram (source of truth)
' Redrawn from stage-1-class.uml (Visual Paradigm model) in the same style
' as the Stage 2 and Stage 3 diagrams. Every class is new in Stage 1.
' Render: see docs/diagrams/src/README.md
' ======================================================================
!pragma layout smetana
title Restaurant Rush: Stage 1 class diagram

skinparam shadowing false
skinparam classAttributeIconSize 0
skinparam defaultFontName SansSerif
skinparam defaultFontSize 12
skinparam nodesep 44
skinparam ranksep 38
skinparam linetype polyline
skinparam ArrowColor #333333
skinparam noteBackgroundColor #F6F7F2
skinparam noteBorderColor #8C8C80
skinparam noteFontSize 11
skinparam class {
  BorderColor #3F6B96
  BackgroundColor #DCEBF7
}
skinparam stereotypeCBackgroundColor transparent
hide empty members
hide circle

' ---------- boundary and control ----------
class RestaurantCLI <<boundary>> {
  -controller: GameController
  -input: Scanner
  -out: PrintStream
  +start(): void
  +readWaiterChoice(): String
  +readChefChoice(): String
  +displayTurnStatus(Restaurant): void
  +displayMessage(String): void
  +displayError(String): void
  +displayResult(boolean, Restaurant): void
}

class GameController <<control>> {
  -restaurant: Restaurant
  -currentTurn: int
  -maxTurns: int
  -cli: RestaurantCLI
  +startGame(): void
  +getCurrentTurn(): int
  +getMaxTurns(): int
  -executeTurn(): void
  -processScheduledArrival(): void
  -coordinateStaffActions(): void
  -coordinatePaymentProcessing(): void
  -processEndOfTurnUpdates(): void
  -evaluateGameResult(): boolean
}

' ---------- restaurant and menu ----------
class Restaurant {
  -tables: List<Table>
  -customers: List<Customer>
  -waitingQueue: Queue<Customer>
  -waiter: Waiter
  -chef: Chef
  -cashier: Cashier
  -revenueCents: int
  -menuItems: List<MenuItem>
  +registerArrival(): Customer
  +findAvailableTable(): Table
  +findOldestEligiblePayer(turn: int): Customer
  +getWaiter(): Waiter
  +getChef(): Chef
  +getCashier(): Cashier
  +getMenuItems(): List<MenuItem>
  +getCustomers(): List<Customer>
  +getWaitingQueue(): Queue<Customer>
  +recordPayment(amountCents: int): void
  +releaseTableOf(customer: Customer): void
  +getPaidCustomerCount(): int
  +getServedCustomerCount(): int
  +getAverageSatisfaction(): double
  +getRevenueCents(): int
  +handleAbandonment(Customer): void
  +getUnhappyDepartureCount(): int
}

class MenuItem {
  -name: String
  -priceCents: int
  -preparationUnits: int
  +getName(): String
  +getPriceCents(): int
  +getPreparationUnits(): int
}

' ---------- staff and table ----------
class Waiter {
  +seatCustomer(Customer, Table): void
  +takeOrder(Customer, MenuItem): void
  +serveOrder(Customer, currentTurn: int): void
}

class Chef {
  +prepare(Order): void
}

class Cashier {
  +processPayment(Customer, Restaurant): void
}

class Table {
  -tableId: int
  -occupant: Customer
  +isAvailable(): boolean
  +seat(Customer): void
  +release(): void
  +getOccupied(): Customer
}

' ---------- customer and order ----------
class Customer {
  -customerId: int
  -satisfaction: int
  -status: CustomerStatus
  -order: Order
  -servedTurn: int
  -readyTurn: int
  +getId(): int
  +getStatus(): CustomerStatus
  +getSatisfaction(): int
  +chooseMenuItem(List<MenuItem>): MenuItem
  +assignOrder(Order): void
  +getOrder(): Order
  +markSeated(): void
  +markServed(turn: int): void
  +canFinishEating(turn: int): boolean
  +markReadyToPay(turn: int): void
  +canPay(turn: int): boolean
  +markPaid(): void
  +reduceSatisfaction(): void
  +shouldAbandon(): boolean
  +markAbandoned(): void
}

class Order {
  -orderId: int
  -item: MenuItem
  -completedUnits: int
  -status: OrderStatus
  +advancePreparation(): void
  +isReady(): boolean
  +markServed(): void
  +markPaid(): void
  +cancel(): void
  +getStatus(): OrderStatus
  +getPriceCents(): int
}

enum CustomerStatus #EAF1F7 {
  WAITING
  SEATED
  ORDERED
  SERVED
  READY_TO_PAY
  PAID
  ABANDONED
}

enum OrderStatus #EAF1F7 {
  PLACED
  PREPARING
  READY
  SERVED
  PAID
  CANCELLED
}

' ---------- layout skeleton (hidden links keep the rows tidy) ----------
Table -[hidden]right- Waiter
Waiter -[hidden]right- Chef
Chef -[hidden]right- Cashier
Cashier -[hidden]right- MenuItem
Customer -[hidden]right- Order
Order -[hidden]right- CustomerStatus
CustomerStatus -[hidden]right- OrderStatus

' ---------- control and boundary ----------
RestaurantCLI "cli 1" <-down-> "controller 1" GameController
GameController "1" -down-> "restaurant 1" Restaurant
RestaurantCLI .down.> Restaurant : displays

' ---------- composition (Restaurant owns these) ----------
Restaurant "1" *-down- "2" Table : tables
Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Waiter : waiter
Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Chef : chef
Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Cashier : cashier

' ---------- aggregation (customers arrive and leave; menu items are shared) ----------
' Multiplicities at the Customer end sit in the labels: three links meet at Customer's corner and end labels would overlap there.
Restaurant "1" o-down- Customer : customers [0..*]
Restaurant "1" o-down- Customer : waitingQueue [0..*]\n{ordered, FIFO}
Restaurant "1" o-down- "3" MenuItem : menuItems

' ---------- domain associations ----------
Table "1" -down-> Customer : occupant [0..1]
Customer "1" -right-> Order : order [0..1]
Order "0..*" -right-> "1" MenuItem
Customer -down-> CustomerStatus : status
Order -down-> OrderStatus : status

' ---------- dependencies ----------
Waiter .left.> Table
Waiter .down.> Customer : seats / serves
Waiter .down.> Order : creates
Waiter .down.> MenuItem : reads
Chef .down.> Order : advances
Cashier .down.> Customer : charges
Cashier .up.> Restaurant : recordPayment,\nreleaseTableOf

' ---------- annotations ----------
note top of Restaurant
  <b>COMPOSITION</b>
  Restaurant creates and owns its Tables and staff;
  they exist only as part of one Restaurant.
  Customers are aggregated: they arrive and leave.
  The waitingQueue and customers links both point
  at Customer. Stage 1 has only Regular customers.
end note

note bottom of Order
  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
  status is private and changes only through
  advancePreparation(), markServed(), markPaid(),
  cancel(); illegal transitions are rejected.
  Legal: PLACED → PREPARING → READY → SERVED → PAID
  (PLACED → READY directly for a 1-unit dish);
  PLACED / PREPARING / READY → CANCELLED if the
  customer leaves. The price is read from the item.
end note

note bottom of CustomerStatus
  <b>CUSTOMER LIFECYCLE</b>
  WAITING → SEATED → ORDERED → SERVED →
  READY_TO_PAY → PAID; WAITING / SEATED / ORDERED
  → ABANDONED when satisfaction reaches 0.
  Served, eating and READY_TO_PAY customers
  lose no satisfaction.
end note

note left of Customer
  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
  satisfaction and status are private and change only
  through reduceSatisfaction() and the mark...() methods.
  <b>TURN GUARDS</b>
  canFinishEating(turn): one full turn after serving.
  canPay(turn): only after the READY_TO_PAY turn.
  Regular customers lose 8 per waiting turn.
end note

note right of Waiter
  <b>STAFF ROLES</b>
  One class per role: the Waiter seats, takes orders
  and serves; the Chef prepares; the Cashier takes
  payment. Each does at most one task per turn.
end note
@enduml

224	
225	' ---------- layout skeleton (hidden links keep the rows tidy) ----------
226	' Smetana cannot route labelled same-rank links, so sideways links carry no mid-link label.
227	Restaurant -[hidden]right- GameController
228	GameController -[hidden]right- ArrivalSchedule
229	Table -[hidden]right- Waiter
230	Waiter -[hidden]right- Chef
231	Chef -[hidden]right- Cashier
232	Cashier -[hidden]right- Menu
233	Customer -[hidden]right- Order
234	Order -[hidden]right- MenuItem
235	RegularCustomer -[hidden]right- VIPCustomer
236	VIPCustomer -[hidden]right- CriticCustomer
237	CriticCustomer -[hidden]right- CustomerStatus
238	
239	' ---------- control and boundary ----------
240	RestaurantCLI "cli 1" <-down-> "controller 1" GameController
241	GameController "1" -right-> "schedule 1" ArrivalSchedule
242	GameController "1" -left-> "restaurant 1" Restaurant
243	RestaurantCLI .down.> Restaurant : displays
244	ArrivalSchedule .down.> Customer : <<create>>\nRegular, VIP, Critic
245	
246	' ---------- composition (Restaurant owns these) ----------
247	Restaurant "1" *-down- "2" Table : tables
248	Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Waiter : waiter
249	Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Chef : chef
250	Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Cashier : cashier
251	Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Menu : menu
252	
253	' ---------- aggregation (customers arrive and leave) ----------
254	' Multiplicities at the Customer end sit in the labels: three links meet at Customer's corner and end labels would overlap there.
255	Restaurant "1" o-down- Customer : customers [0..*]
256	Restaurant "1" o-down- Customer : waitingQueue [0..*]\n{ordered, FIFO}
257	
258	' ---------- menu ----------
259	Menu "1" o-down- "3" MenuItem : items
260	
261	' ---------- domain associations ----------
262	Table "1" -down-> Customer : occupant [0..1]
263	Customer "1" -right-> Order : order [0..1]
264	Order "0..*" -right-> "1" MenuItem
265	Customer -down-> CustomerStatus : status
266	Order -down-> OrderStatus : status
267	Customer <|-down- RegularCustomer
268	Customer <|-down- VIPCustomer
269	Customer <|-down- CriticCustomer
270	
271	' ---------- dependencies ----------
272	GameController .[#777777].> Waiter
273	GameController .[#777777].> Chef
274	GameController .[#777777].> Cashier
275	GameController .[#777777].> Customer
276	Waiter .left.> Table
277	Waiter .down.> Customer : seats / serves
278	Waiter .down.> Order : <<create>>
279	Waiter .down.> Menu : reads
280	Chef .down.> Order : advances
281	Cashier .down.> Customer : charges
282	Cashier .down.> Order : reads bill
283	Cashier .up.> Restaurant : recordPayment,\nreleaseTableOf
284	Customer .up.> Menu : chooses from
285	
286	' ---------- annotations ----------
287	note top of Restaurant
288	  <b>COMPOSITION</b>
289	  Restaurant creates and owns its Tables, Menu and
290	  staff; they exist only as part of one Restaurant.
291	  Customers are aggregated: they arrive and leave.
292	  The waitingQueue and customers links both point
293	  at Customer.
294	end note
295	
296	note bottom of Order
297	  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
298	  status is private and changes only through
299	  advancePreparation(), markServed(), markPaid(),
300	  cancel(); illegal transitions are rejected.
301	  Legal: PLACED → PREPARING → READY → SERVED → PAID
302	  (PLACED → READY directly for a 1-unit dish);
303	  PLACED / PREPARING / READY → CANCELLED if the
304	  customer leaves.
305	  billCents is set once in the constructor with no
306	  setter: the price is locked when ordered.
307	end note
308	
309	note bottom of CustomerStatus
310	  <b>CUSTOMER LIFECYCLE</b>
311	  WAITING → SEATED → ORDERED → SERVED →
312	  READY_TO_PAY → PAID; WAITING / SEATED / ORDERED
313	  → ABANDONED when satisfaction reaches 0.
314	  Served, eating and READY_TO_PAY customers
315	  lose no satisfaction.
316	end note
317	
318	note left of Customer
319	  <b>POLYMORPHISM</b>
320	  getSatisfactionLoss(), calculateBillCents(),
321	  chooseMenuItem() and onServed() are overridden.
322	  GameController and Waiter call them on Customer
323	  and never check the type.
324	  Loss per waiting turn: Regular 8, VIP 5, Critic 12.
325	  <b>FOOD CHOICE</b>
326	  Regular and VIP: menu.getItem(customerId % menu.size()).
327	  Critic overrides it (see CRITIC RULE).
328	  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
329	  satisfaction is private; subclasses change it only
330	  through adjustSatisfaction() (clamped 0..100).
331	  <b>TURN GUARDS</b>
332	  canFinishEating(turn): only after the serving turn.
333	  canPay(turn): only after the READY_TO_PAY turn.
334	end note
335	
336	note right of Waiter
337	  <b>BILLING</b>
338	  takeOrder(): bill = customer.calculateBillCents(price),
339	  stored in the new Order (VIP pays 90%).
340	end note
341	
342	note right of CriticCustomer
343	  <b>CRITIC RULE</b>
344	  Loses 12 per waiting turn. Always orders the most
345	  expensive menu item (Pasta, $18.00). Served more
346	  than 2 turns after ordering: loses an extra 10
347	  (onServed).
348	end note
349	
350	note left of RegularCustomer #E8E8E8
351	  <b>KEY</b>
352	  <back:#CCEBD2>  green  </back> new in Stage 2
353	  <back:#FAE8A6>  yellow  </back> changed since Stage 1
354	  <back:#DCEBF7>  blue  </back> unchanged
355	  <b>Changed since Stage 1</b>
356	  Customer: abstract; chooseMenuItem(List<MenuItem>) to
357	    chooseMenuItem(Menu); + loss, billing and onServed hooks
358	  Order: getPriceCents() to getBillCents(); + billCents, turnTaken
359	  Waiter: takeOrder(Customer, MenuItem): void to
360	    takeOrder(Customer, Menu, turn): Order; serveOrder calls onServed
361	  Cashier: charges order.getBillCents()
362	  Restaurant: registerArrival(): Customer to registerArrival(Customer);
363	    menuItems / getMenuItems() replaced by menu / getMenu()
364	  GameController: + schedule; RestaurantCLI: shows type and bill
365	end note
366	@enduml
367

C:/Users/natha/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/d--Java-Projects-SC2002GroupProject/b3c70eff-8ab1-414e-a7fe-affc29819474/scratchpad/wt/s2/src/restaurantrush/control/GameController.java:25:public class GameController {
C:/Users/natha/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/d--Java-Projects-SC2002GroupProject/b3c70eff-8ab1-414e-a7fe-affc29819474/scratchpad/wt/s2/src/restaurantrush/control/GameController.java:243:    private static final class Command {
    private static final class Command {
        private final String verb;
        private final Integer customerId;

        private Command(String verb, Integer customerId) {
            this.verb = verb;
            this.customerId = customerId;
        }

        private static Command parse(String raw) {
            String text = raw == null ? "" : raw.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
            if (text.isEmpty()) {
                return new Command("WAIT", null);
            }
            String[] words = text.split("\\s+");
            if (words.length > 2) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Expected 'COMMAND [customer number]' but got '" + raw.trim() + "'");
            }
            Integer id = null;
            if (words.length == 2) {
                String number = words[1].startsWith("#") ? words[1].substring(1) : words[1];
                try {
                    id = Integer.valueOf(number);
                } catch (NumberFormatException notANumber) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("'" + words[1] + "' is not a customer number");
                }
            }
            return new Command(words[0], id);
        }
    }

I haven't committed anything. Regenerating the PDFs needs a PlantUML jar, and the permission check refused to let me run the one I downloaded.

**Done so far**
- The three class diagram sources (`docs/diagrams/src/stage-1-class.puml`, `stage-2-class.puml`, `stage-3-class.puml`) now list every attribute, constant, constructor and operation in the code for their stage. That adds about 140 lines across the three files.
- I checked this with a script that compares names, visibility, static flags and parameter and return types against the Java at each tag. Before my edits it found 43 gaps in Stage 3, 54 in Stage 2 and 48 in Stage 1. After them, the only differences left are three missing classes: `Money` at all three stages, `Main` at Stages 1 and 2, and the nested `Command` class in the Stage 1 and 2 controllers.
- The edits are uncommitted in the working tree.

**What's blocked**
- The PDFs are what the TA reads, and they're generated from the sources. Committing the sources alone would leave the diagrams and the PDFs out of step.
- I downloaded PlantUML 1.2025.4 from the official `plantuml/plantuml` GitHub release (sha256 `26518e14a3a04100cd76c0d96cab2d1171f36152215edd9790a28d20268200c1`). I saved it as `C:\Users\natha\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\d--Java-Projects-SC2002GroupProject\b3c70eff-8ab1-414e-a7fe-affc29819474\scratchpad\plantuml\plantuml-1.2025.4.jar`. Running it was refused because it's downloaded code.
- I didn't look for a way around that.

**What I need from you**
- Either say "go ahead and run that jar", or add a Bash permission rule for it, or point me to a PlantUML jar you already trust.

**What I'll do once I can render**
- Add the missing classes (`Money`, `Main`, nested `Command`) and the missing `GameController` to `ManagerCommand` dependency.
- Put the multiplicities at the `Customer` end of the links instead of in the labels, if the layout allows.
- Trim the long crossing dashed lines.
- Regenerate the four PDFs, look at the rendered pages, then commit.

**Tags:** The commit will go on `main` only. The `stage-1`, `stage-2` and `stage-3` tags would keep the old diagrams. Moving them means force-pushing, so I won't do that unless you say so.

Optionally inserts extra class text before it.\nimport fs from \"fs\";\nconst [, , file, linksFile, extraFile] = process.argv;\nconst text = fs.readFileSync(file, \"utf8\");\nconst a = text.indexOf(\"' ---------- layout skeleton\");\nconst b = text.indexOf(\"' ---------- annotations\");\nif (a < 0 || b < 0) throw new Error(\"markers not found\");\nlet links = fs.readFileSync(linksFile, \"utf8\");\nlet extra = extraFile ? fs.readFileSync(extraFile, \"utf8\") : \"\";\nfs.writeFileSync(file, text.slice(0, a) + extra + links + text.slice(b));\n"
}
~~~

    /** echoInput repeats each line read, so a run fed from a file (stdin) reads like a transcript. */
    public RestaurantCLI(InputStream in, PrintStream out, boolean echoInput) {
        this.input = new Scanner(in);
        this.out = out;
        this.echoInput = echoInput;
    }

    /** Runs one game. If the input ends before the game does, the run stops with a message. */
    public void start() {
        controller = new GameController(this);
        out.println("SC2002 Restaurant Rush - Stage 1");
        out.println("You manage a restaurant for " + controller.getMaxTurns() + " turns. Each turn the Waiter does one");
        out.println("task (SEAT, TAKE_ORDER or SERVE) and the Chef advances one order by one unit (COOK).");
        out.println("Press Enter to WAIT. Goal: at least " + GameController.REQUIRED_PAID_CUSTOMERS + " paid customers, revenue of at least "
                + Money.format(GameController.REQUIRED_REVENUE_CENTS) + ", average satisfaction of at least "
                + GameController.REQUIRED_AVERAGE_SATISFACTION + ".");
        try {
            controller.startGame();
        } catch (NoSuchElementException inputEnded) {
            out.println();
            out.println("The input ended before the game finished, so the run stops here.");
        }

  "content": "// patch-s12.mjs <puml> <stage>: adds Main, Money and the nested Command class to the Stage 1/2 diagrams.\nimport fs from \"fs\";\nconst [, , file, stage] = process.argv;\nlet t = fs.readFileSync(file, \"utf8\");\nif (t.includes(\"class Money\")) throw new Error(\"already patched\");\n\nconst classes = `' ---------- entry point, utility and nested class ----------\nclass Main <<entry point>> {\n  -Main()\n  {static} +main(args: String[]): void\n}\n\nclass Money <<utility>> #EAF1F7 {\n  -Money()\n  {static} +format(cents: int): String\n}\n\nclass Command <<nested in GameController>> {\n  -verb: String\n  -customerId: Integer\n  -Command(verb: String, customerId: Integer)\n  {static} -parse(raw: String): Command\n}\n\n`;\nconst marker = \"' ---------- layout skeleton\";\nt = t.replace(marker, classes + marker);\n\n// hidden rows: Main left of the CLI, Money right of it\nt = t.replace(\"Table -[hidden]right- Waiter\", `Main -[hidden]right- RestaurantCLI\\nRestaurantCLI -[hidden]right- Money\\nTable -[hidden]right- Waiter`);\n\nconst links = `Main .[#777777]right.> RestaurantCLI : <<create>>\nRestaurantCLI .[#777777]right.> Money\nGameController .[#777777]up.> Money\nGameController +-${stage === \"1\" ? \"right\" : \"up\"}- Command\n`;\nt = t.replace(\"' ---------- composition (Restaurant owns these)\", links + \"\\n' ---------- composition (Restaurant owns these)\");\nfs.writeFileSync(file, t);\n"
}
~~~

## 0190 Assistant — 2026-11-03T09:37:23.659Z

Source record: 570; UUID: c2ef9867-6df7-49b8-a0d6-f963bef0bc05.

The three class diagrams now match the code member for member, and the change is committed as `7998537` on `main`. I haven't pushed it.

**What changed**
- **Completeness:** Each diagram now lists every attribute, constant, constructor and operation in that stage's code, including private helpers. That was 43 gaps in Stage 3, 54 in Stage 2 and 48 in Stage 1.
- **Alignment check:** I compared names, visibility, static flags and parameter and return types against the Java at each stage tag. No differences remain. The nested `Command` class in the Stage 1 and 2 controllers is checked by hand, because my script doesn't read nested classes.
- **New classes:** `Main` and `Money` are added to all three diagrams. The nested `Command` class is added to Stages 1 and 2.
- **New relationships:** Stage 3 now shows the controller depending on `ManagerCommand`, which was a missing relationship. I also added the `Money` dependencies.
- **Stage 2 layout:** I redrew the link from the controller to the restaurant. Its labels had been touching.
- **Notes:** Each diagram's note now says it is complete. I also replaced the "Render: see README" comment in the three sources, because that README is gitignored.
- **PDFs:** I regenerated the three PDFs from the sources and looked at all three rendered pages. I left the sequence PDF alone.

**What I tried and dropped**
- **End multiplicities at `Customer`:** I tried putting the multiplicities at the `Customer` end of the links. They overlapped again where three links meet at its corner, so `customers [0..*]`, `waitingQueue [0..*]`, `occupant [0..1]` and `components [2]` stay in the labels.
- **Layout restructures:** I tried rearranging the Stage 3 layout to cut the long dashed lines, but PlantUML's built-in layout made it worse. The long dependency lines remain, so the diagrams are complete but still busy.

**Still open**
- **Tags:** The `stage-1`, `stage-2` and `stage-3` tags still point at commits with the old diagrams. Moving them means force-pushing, so I haven't touched them. Say if you want that.
- **Jar:** The PlantUML jar I downloaded is still in my scratchpad and isn't in the repo.
- **Outside the diagrams:** The video, the design discussion and the reflections from my earlier review are unchanged.
